Management Discussion and Analysis

回顧與展望                        REVIEW AND PROSPECTS

2024年上半年，房地產政策主基調延續寬鬆，       In the first half of 2024, the main tone of policies on real estate continued
政策側重點轉向「去庫存」。在需求端，居民信        to be relaxed, with the policy focus shifting to “destocking”. In terms of
貸政策迎來利好，多數城市取消限購與按揭貸         demand side, credit policies for residents have ushered in positive results.
款利率下限，調降首套房與二套房貸款首付比         Most cities have lifted purchase restrictions and lowered the minimum
例。在供給端，房地產融資協調機制建立並加         requirement of the interest rates on mortgage loans, and reduced the
速推進，央行推出再貸款工具支持地方國資收         down payment ratio for the loans on the first and second housing units. In
儲存量商品房，各地推進住房「以舊換新」。相        terms of supply side, a real estate financing coordination mechanism was
信政策會給予市場持續支撐，指明方向。           established and its implementation has been accelerated. The central
                             bank has launched refinancing tools to support local state-owned assets
                             committees in purchasing and storing existing commercial housing, and
                             local governments have promoted “old for new” housing schemes. It is
                             believed that the policy will provide continuous support to the market and
                             point out the direction.

集團」）繼續堅守保交付、保信用的目標，同         Group”) continued to adhere to pursuing the goal of guaranteeing

財務安全是本集團發展的基石，新城始終堅守                      The Group regards financial safety as the cornerstone of its development.
嚴格的財務鐵律，完成了公開市場到期債務的                      Seazen always adheres to the strict financial discipline, and has
及時與安全兌付。本集團在融資方面獲得了金                      completed timely and safety payment of debts due on the open market.
融機構的大力支持，融資渠道保持暢通。2024                    The Group has received strong supports from financial institutions with
年5月，本集團子公司新城控股集團股份有限                      respect to financing to ensure a smooth provision of financial facilities. In
公司（「新城控股」）成功發行人民幣13.6億元中                  May 2024, Seazen Holdings Co., Ltd. (“Seazen Holdings”), a subsidiary
期票據。新城將始終把安全性放於一切發展的                      of the Group, has successfully issued medium term notes of RMB1.36
首位，持續優化債務結構，確保財務底線安                       billion. Seazen will always prioritise financial safety over the development,
全。                                        and continue to optimise its debt structure, in a bid to ensure a healthy
                                          net profit.

本集團在環境、社會及管治（「ESG」）方面表現                   The Group achieved brilliant results in terms of environmental, social
優秀，2024年1月，新城發展獲明晟MSCI ESG                and governance (“ESG”), with MSCI ESG rating upgraded from BB
評級由BB級提升至BBB級。新城踐行可持續發                    to BBB in January 2024. For the purpose of sustainability, Seazen
展理念，推進「新藍行動」，希望通過構建綠色                     promoted the idea of “New Blue Action” and made green development

降低外匯風險。                                   scale of assets and liabilities in foreign currencies to minimize the foreign
                                          exchange risks.

股份獎勵計劃                                    SHARE AWARD SCHEME

於2019年11月1日，董事會決議採納股份獎勵                   On 1 November 2019, the Board has resolved to adopt a share award
計劃（「股份獎勵計劃」）。股份獎勵計劃的宗旨                    scheme (the “Share Award Scheme”). The objectives of the Share
為：(i)肯定本公司僱員（「僱員」）所作出的貢獻                  Award Scheme are: (i) to recognize the contribution of employees of the
及獎勵僱員對本集團未來的長期發展繼續作出                      Company (the “Employees”) and to provide incentives for the Employees
重大貢獻；及(ii)吸引及挽留可能對本集團成長                   to continuously make substantial contributions for the Group’s long-term
及發展有益的優秀僱員。                               growth in the future; and (ii) to attract and retain talented Employees who
                                          may be beneficial to the growth and development of the Group.

 董事會就管理股份獎勵計劃而委任的受託人                      The aggregated maximum number of Shares that the trustee (who is an
（「受託人」）
      （為獨立第三方）可購買的最高股份                    independent third party) appointed by the Board to manage the Share
 數目累計不得超過本公司於2019年11月1日全                  Award Scheme (the “Trustee”) may purchase must not exceed 0.5%
 部已發行股本的0.5%，即29,495,000股股份               of the total share capital in issue of the Company on 1 November 2019,
（佔本報告日期本公司已發行股份約0.42%）。                   i.e. 29,495,000 shares (representing approximately 0.42% of the issued
                                          shares of the Company as at the date of this report).

除非董事會提前終止，否則股份獎勵計劃的有                      Unless early terminated by the Board, the Share Award Scheme shall be
效期為自2019年11月1日採納日期起至2029年                 effective for ten years from the adoption date of 1 November 2019 and
10月31日止十年。於本報告日期，股份獎勵計                    up to 31 October 2029. As at the date of this report, the remaining life of
劃的剩餘年期約為五年又兩個月。                           the Share Award Scheme was around five years and two months.

    Cayman Islands。

    業務為於中華人民共和國（「中國」）從事物                       the “Group”) are property development and property investment in
    業開發及物業投資。本公司的母公司為富                         the People’s Republic of China (the “PRC”). The Company’s parent
    域香港投資有限公司（「富域香港」），本公                       company is Wealth Zone Hong Kong Investments Limited (“Wealth

         本集團的業務承受多項財務風險：市                     The Group’s activities expose it to a variety of financial risks:
         場風險（包括貨幣風險、價格風險、                     market risk (including currency risk, price risk, cash flow interest
         現金流量利率風險及公允價值利率風                     rate risk and fair value interest rate risk), credit risk and liquidity
         險）、信貸風險及流動資金風險。本集                    risk. The Group’s overall risk management program focuses on
         團的整體風險管理計劃專注於金融市                     the unpredictability of financial markets and seeks to minimize
         場的不可預測性，並尋求方法盡量降                     potential adverse effects on the Group’s financial performances.
         低對本集團財務業績可能構成的不利
         影響。

    5.4 資本風險管理                                          5.4 Capital risk management

         本集團的資本管理目標為保障本集團                                  The Group’s objectives when managing capital are to safeguard
         能繼續營運，以為股東提供回報和維                                  the Group’s ability to continue as a going concern in order to
         持最佳的資本結構以降低資金成本。                                  provide returns for shareholders and to maintain an optimal
                                                           capital structure to reduce the cost of capital.

         為維持或調整資本結構，本集團可能                                  In order to maintain or adjust the capital structure, the Group
         會調整支付予股東的股息金額、發行                                  may adjust the amount of dividends paid to shareholders, issue
         新股份或出售資產以減少債務。                                    new shares or sell assets to reduce debt.

         本集團利用資產負債比率監察其資                                   The Group monitors capital on the basis of the gearing ratio.
         本。此比率按照債務淨額除以資本總                                  This ratio is calculated as net debt divided by total capital. Net
         額計算。債務淨額按借款總額及租賃                                  debt is calculated as total borrowings plus lease liabilities less
         負債減現金及現金等價物計算。資本                                  cash and cash equivalents. Total capital is calculated as total
         總額按合併財務狀況表所列示的權益                                  equity, as shown in the consolidated statement of financial
         總額加上債務淨額計算。                                       position, plus net debt.

         於 2024 年 6 月 30 日 及 2023 年 12 月 31                The gearing ratios of the Group at 30 June 2024 and 31
         日，本集團資產負債比率如下：                                    December 2023 were as follows:

6 分部資料                                    6 SEGMENT INFORMATION

    管理層根據主要經營決策者（「主要經營決                     Management has determined the operating segments based on the
    策者」）審核的報告（用於作出戰略決策）釐                    reports reviewed by the chief operating decision maker (the “CODM”)
    定經營分部。董事會被視為主要經營決策                      that are used to make strategic decisions. The board of directors
    者。                                      have been identified as the CODM.

    本集團透過兩個經營分部管理業務，其與                      The Group manages its business by two operating segments, which
    就資源分配及表現評估目的向本集團主要                      is consistent with the way in which information is reported internally
    經營決策者進行內部資料報告的方式一                       to the Group’s CODM for the purpose of resources allocation
    致。本集團概無合併經營分部以組成下列                      and performance assessment. No operating segments have been
    可呈報分部。                                  aggregated to form the following reportable segments.

     •   新城控股集團股份有限公司，一家在                   •   Seazen Holdings Co., Ltd., a company listed on the Shanghai
         上海證券交易所上市的公司（「A股公                      Stock Exchange (the “A share company” or “Seazen Holdings”).
         司」或「新城控股」）。

    本集團就將予開發及在建投資物業採用直                      For investment properties to be developed and under development,
    接比較法及投資法。直接比較法乃假設該                      the Group adopted direct comparison approach and investment
    等物業各自按現況交吉出售。經參考有關                      approach. Direct comparison approach is assuming sale of each
    市場的可資比較銷售交易，選擇鄰近地區                      of these properties in its existing state with the benefit of vacant
    的可資比較物業並就位置及物業規模等因                      possession. By making reference to sales transactions as available
    素的差異作出調整。投資法計及已產生建                      in the relevant market, comparable properties in close proximity have
    設成本及完成該開發項目將產生的成本以                      been selected and adjustments have been made to account for the
    反映已竣工開發項目的質量，依據是該等                      difference in factors such as locations and property size. Investment
    物業將根據本集團最近開發計劃予以開發                      approach has taken into account the construction costs incurred
    和完成。就已竣工投資物業而言，估值乃                      and the costs that will be incurred to complete the development to
    根據投資法透過將來自現有租約的淨租金                      reflect the quality of the completed development on the basis that
    收入（在適當計及物業復歸潛在收入後）作                     the properties will be developed and completed in accordance with
    出資本化計算，並經參考於活躍市場上的                      the Group’s latest development plan. For completed investment
    可比較價格後作出。                               properties, valuations were based on investment approach by
                                            capitalisation of the net rental income derived from the existing
                                            tenancy agreements with due allowance for reversionary income
                                            potential of the properties and by reference to comparable price in
                                            an active market.

    期內估值方法並無變動。                             There were no changes to the valuation techniques during the

         就本集團物業若干買家的                    Guarantees in respect of mortgage
          按揭融資作出的擔保                      facilities for certain purchasers of
                                         the Group’s properties                               29,568,441         33,026,298


         本集團已為本集團物業若干買家安排                                   The Group has arranged bank financing for certain purchasers
         銀行融資，並就買家的還款責任提供                                   of the Group’s properties and provided guarantees to secure
         擔保。該等擔保將於下列較早者終                                    obligations of such purchaser for repayments. Such guarantees
         止：(i)房地產所有權證由政府部門發出                                will terminate upon the earlier of (i) the issue of the real estate
         予買家，此證一般在擔保登記完成後                                   ownership certificate by government authorities to the purchaser
         平均兩至三年內，並向按揭銀行提交                                   which will generally occur within an average period of two to
         時發出；或(ii)物業買家清償按揭貸款                                three years from the completion of the guarantee registration
         時。                                                 and submitted to the mortgage bank; or (ii) the satisfaction of
                                                            mortgage loans by the purchasers of the properties.

         根據擔保條款，在該等買家拖欠按揭                                   Pursuant to the terms of the guarantees, upon default of
         還款時，本集團須負責向銀行償還違                                   mortgage payments by these purchasers, the Group is
         約買家拖欠的尚未償還按揭本金連同                                   responsible to repay the outstanding mortgage principal
         應計利息及罰金，而本集團有權接管                                   together with accrued interest and penalties owed by the
         相關物業的法定業權及所有權。本集                                   defaulting purchasers to the banks and the Group is entitled to
         團的擔保期由授出按揭日期起開始。                                   take over the legal title and possession of the related properties.
         董事認為買家拖欠付款的可能性極                                    The Group’s guarantee period starts from the date of grant of
         小，因此，按公允價值計量的財務擔                                   mortgage. The directors consider that the likelihood of default
         保並不重大。                                             of payments by the purchasers is minimal and therefore the
                                                            financial guarantee measured at fair value is immaterial.
